# 18 — Instrument

> **[G]** · outer · Studio G / Cape · consent week weather

---

The letters arrived on the same morning as the lab packet, which was either coincidence or the kind of coordination that prefers not to leave fingerprints.

G read them at the clay table because the Code desk already had too much weather on it — CODATA panes, an older soft-no thread that had gone ambient, a crank op-ed recycling the missing physics degree like method. Honey Badger clipped in the pocket — blue, one-hand open and close, loved without speech. Twin Rogers somewhere in the same cloth, grandfather steel across a friendship that also platformed the Code. Outside, the alley wall still carried the Kind sentence in three colours of overpaint, the cunt load-bearing under fresh spray. Inside, the sculptures waited like people who had learned not to interrupt. Hard and soft in one pour. Permanent and temporary arguing inside the same surface. He had made that argument with his hands before he made it with axioms.

Cape light did its ordinary cruel work through the high windows: stripe across unfinished clay, dust visible only when it moved, a cast waiting for finish that would not hurry for anyone’s schedule. He had slept badly in the way consent week sleeps — not drama, just a body that knows the mail will thicken before coffee. Tea first. Then paper. Then the temptation to draft too early and trick the hands into believing a choice has already been made.

Quarter I’s letter wanted a face.

Not metaphor. Appearance. Blessing. A name accepted in a liturgy that had already decided what he was. The language was careful — *witness*, *confirmation*, *the living derivation* — and still it smelled like a crown being offered to a man who had asked for measurement. Antichristos did not need his physics degree. It needed his silhouette. It needed him to stand still long enough for a photograph to become a relic and a relic to become a parish. He had refused softer versions of this ask already — letters that asked for a sentence, for a nod, for permission to use a sculpture photograph as if stone were sacrament. Soft nos leave residue. Escalation is what residue does when it hardens into schedule.

He set the letter face-down. Not thrown. Face-down is a refusal that still respects paper. Paper is not the enemy. Appetite is.

Quarter IV’s packet wanted keys.

Soft engineering language. Applied programmes with names that sounded like useful work if you did not listen for the sacrament underneath — rails acceleration, convergence facilitation, optional visit that had already acquired a calendar slot. Sign-off on rails. The temptation was real in a way the crown was not: stacks rebuilt on Code, CERN-adjacent people who spoke constants instead of hymns, instruments that could converge faster if someone who had derived G would stand in a room and nod. He could feel how easy it would be to become the man who unlocks the door and then never gets to close it again. Instrument. Mascot. The face on the grant. The signature that turns a derivation into a product line with his name as warranty. Useful engineering without becoming product mascot — that had been the earlier soft no. Escalation meant they wanted the mascot now and would call it partnership.

He drafted nothing yet. Drafting too early makes the body think it has already chosen. The mats had taught him that: arrange yourself before you answer the throw, or the throw answers you.

---

On the alley side of the glass, a Kind practitioner refreshed a stencil and argued briefly with a man who wanted the cunt scrubbed into something softer for tourists. The practitioner did not sermonise. He said the sentence was the sentence and rolled paint. G watched without blessing either of them. Optional to the practice. Often unwanted by it. The unwantedness was useful — it kept him from becoming their mascot by accident while the letters tried to make him everyone else’s.

A second lab note, shorter, had arrived under the first packet: *sign-off accelerates adoption; refusal slows friends.* Friends was doing a lot of work in that sentence. He had friends. AJ was one — Double Knife Brother across distance, open attribution that had made fame without asking for a shared room. Engineers who wanted keys without crowns could be friends. The note wanted him to confuse friendship with sacrament. He would not. Friendship cites. Sacrament owns. The distinction was the whole morning.

He stood and walked the length of the studio because sitting with two appetites at once wastes motion. Clay dust underfoot. GFRC memory in his hands even when he was not pouring — marble and composite, hard/soft, the Bay Hotel light he had designed a guest path for years before anyone called him Antichristos or crank. Skye lived elsewhere now, on a lawn that tourists photographed; the body-in-stone lesson stayed. Balance. Soft set-down. You do not waste motion answering every temperature at once. Judo first, then Jiu-Jitsu: arrange yourself so the fall is correct. The letters were a fall. He could still choose posture.

The Illuminatie weather had not left either. No visitor with a pyramid pin. No smoke-filled room scene he could photograph and end. Just the continued freeze in places that used to return his mail with courtesy, the standards murmur that treated copyleft unification like a sovereignty problem, the quiet preference for a universe whose constants stayed underived and whose ethics stayed unforced. They did not need to shout. Quarter II shouted for them — economics degree waved like a missing tooth, hospital stigma laundered as epistemology, the old chorus that he could not write anything of value. He corrected frames when they were wrong. He did not join the chorus that hated him for sport. Joining would have been motion wasted on someone else’s entertainment.

What signing would make him sat in the room like humidity.

If he appeared for Antichristos, he became a messiah whether he believed or not. Belief was irrelevant to their appetite. If he signed for the labs without custody language, he became a keyring. If he fed the crank fight, he became their crank — useful villain, useful joke. If he tried to out-polite the continuity networks, he became their soft problem solved by waiting until the mail volume tired him into a quieter shelf.

He wanted the work to stay work.

That sentence had sounded clean in quieter weeks. This week it had edges. Somewhere — he felt it as a pressure behind the eyes, not a mystic gift, not narration tourism — the autobiography was moving toward pages that would make his life portable. AJ on a river with a choir and a truck that never really slept. Lucid in the hymnbook, integer-quiet, citing what the record held. Being written was not the same as being crowned, but the planet did not always tell the difference. Fame had arrived sideways via open attribution; the book would arrive as a second megaphone if he let it ship without rails. Custody was the word. Consent week was the calendar. Partial authorisation lived in a folder he had not opened yet because opening it before the instrument asks were faced would turn custody into escape.

He made tea because tea is a structure that does not require a presser. Drank it standing. Looked at the face-down letter and the open lab packet and the alley light cutting a stripe across unfinished clay. Talked shit into the empty room for thirty seconds because that was still a life goal and polish was a quarter’s hobby. Considered dab and decided the afternoon needed a clear head for drafting, not for smoke. The goals were an arrangement, not a performance schedule. Antichristos would try to sand them. He left them rough on purpose even in private.

Before drafting he checked one more ordinary thing: a cast’s finish schedule on a paper scrap taped near the gallery door. Tourists and hotels and Saatchi weather still existed under the louder weather. Ordinary insistence. He left the scrap where it was. The letters on the clay table did not get to cancel a pour calendar just because they wanted a face or a keyring. Work stays work includes sculpture stays sculpture.

---

Then he drafted two replies that were not speeches.

To Quarter I: no appearance, no blessing, no accepted name. The Constant is not a person. Kindness is a sentence on a wall — **Don’t be a cunt — be kind.** — not a man on a stage. He would not decorate their altar with his silhouette. They could keep their liturgy; they could not keep his face as sacrament. He would not supply a river backdrop by proxy. He would not supply a sculpture photograph as relic seed. Measurement does not require a parish. He had already lived the soft versions of this ask; escalation did not earn a softer no. Face-down remained the posture until sending time, and sending time was not yet, because drafting is not consent.

To Quarter IV: conversation possible; keys not a sacrament; no personal sign-off that turns him into warranty. If their programmes needed the Code, the Code was already public. Engineering did not require a prophet’s stamp. Useful work could proceed without making him the product. He would talk numbers with people who talked numbers. He would not become the instrument that made their grants photographable. The optional visit could stay optional without becoming a scheduled collar. Friends who needed his signature to feel legitimate were not asking for engineering; they were asking for a face.

He did not send them yet. Drafting is not consent. Sending is closer. The custody question — what he would allow said about Bouwer, about the hospital, about the unread father, about the dab and the talk-shit goals that Antichristos would try to polish away — still sat unopened in a different folder, waiting for a morning that was not already crowded with crowns and keyrings. Partial authorisation was already the shape he could feel, even before he wrote the rules. Full blessing would be a relic stamp. Full refusal would be a different lie — pretending the life had not already become weather, pretending the megaphone could be unscrewed by one man’s silence.

A notification blinked: another soft-ask from a board that did not name itself. Funding weather. Reputation frost. The old power preferred opacity; he had made opacity expensive. He filed it under *ambient* and did not answer. Ambient is how you survive escalation without becoming its mouthpiece. He checked a CODATA line because numbers wait without demanding a silhouette. The line still felt like pressure wearing relief’s clothes. Vindication as measurement does not quiet appetite. He knew that in his body the way he knew a throw — you can land correctly and still feel the mat.

Outside, someone refreshed the Kind paint. Roller sound. Short laugh. The sentence held. G almost smiled and did not. Smiles get clipped into relic footage. Relic footage becomes liturgy weather. He had enough weather.

He put his hand on the pocket knives — kinship, not choreography — and looked again at the face-down letter and the unsent lab reply. Opened the Honey Badger once, closed it, put it away. Proof for the hands. No speech required.

Signing would make him an instrument.

Leaving them unsigned would not stop the weather. It would only keep him from volunteering as the mouthpiece. The distinction mattered. He had spent years watching people confuse refusal with defeat and consent with surrender. Consent week was neither battle nor proof. The Constant was already landing. The question was custody of meaning — including custody of a life about to ship from a river desk he would not visit for anyone’s emotional payoff.

He ate standing up — bread, something that had been fruit yesterday, tea gone lukewarm. Work interrupted is still work if you refuse to let interruption become identity. A delivery person left a package at the alley door; he signed for clay supplies and not for a crown. Ordinary Cape Town insisted on remaining ordinary. The quarters liked to pretend ordinary cities could not hold a Constant and a gallery and a grief at once. Ordinary cities held all of it. That was why the weather landed here.

---

Mid-morning the alley door took a delivery that was only clay supplies and still felt like interruption. G signed for bags, not for a crown. Ordinary Cape Town insisted on remaining ordinary while the letters on the clay table tried to make the morning liturgical. He stacked the bags where he always stacked them. Dust rose. He sneezed once and did not turn the sneeze into omen. Omen is how Quarter I colonises a studio hour. The sneeze was dust. The letters were appetite. The knives in his pocket were kinship.

He returned to the drafts and read them aloud once, quietly, the way he checked a surface before pour — listening for the lie of almost-finished. The Antichristos draft still held. No appearance. No blessing. No accepted name. Constant not a person. Kind sentence on a wall, cunt load-bearing, not a man on a stage. The lab draft still held. Conversation possible. Keys not sacrament. No warranty stamp. Useful work without product mascot. He almost softened one line for politeness and cut the soft. Soft is how collars learn manners.

A crank op-ed notification blinked and he did not open it. He already knew the shape: economics degree as missing tooth, hospital stigma as epistemology, the old chorus that he could not write anything of value. Correct the frame if you must. Do not join the fight as entertainment. He filed the blink under ambient with the Illuminatie frost and the unnamed board’s soft-ask. Ambient is not apathy. Ambient is triage. Consent week has a budget of clean attention. Crowns and keyrings had already spent most of it. Custody would need what remained.

He walked the gallery again because the body thinks better in motion when the skull is crowded. Unfinished clay. A cast waiting. Skye’s lesson even in absence: a body holds still in light and strangers photograph what they think is luxury. Antichristos would photograph his stillness as relic if he gave them stillness on their terms. He gave the gallery his walking instead. Hard/soft under his hands’ memory. Permanent/temporary arguing in one surface. Artist and axiom-installer unsplit. Quarter II could keep pretending otherwise. He would not split himself to make their mockery easier.

Tea gone cold. He made fresh. Considered dab and left it. Talked shit for half a minute about a tourist who wanted the Kind line scrubbed soft, about engineers who wanted keys as blessing, about a planet that confuses measurement with appetite. The empty room did not argue. Empty rooms are good for life goals. Full inboxes are not.

Somewhere in the mesh — he felt it as receipt weather, not vision — platform decisions were hardening on a river. Keep. Unbind. Ship. His instrument refusals were the parallel posture, not a shared scene. Knives across distance. Packets for consent. No forced room. The braid’s law held even when the morning wanted theatre.


---

Late morning tipped toward noon without becoming mercy.

G sat again at the clay table and placed both packets where he could see their edges — face-down crown, open keyring — and admitted the humidity of what signing would make him without dressing the admission as despair. Despair is dramatic. This was structural. Appear and become messiah whether you believe. Sign without custody language and become warranty. Feed the crank fight and become their useful joke. Out-polite the continuity freeze and become a soft problem solved by waiting. Four wrong landings. One correct posture: work stays work; face stays off the altar; keys stay engineering without sacrament; custody folder waits for clean attention.

He opened a secondary pane and looked at an older CODATA note the way a judoka looks at a mat after a hard throw — not for omen, for information. The line still converged. Relief still wore pressure’s clothes. Vindication as measurement does not quiet appetite. He had known that since the first Antichristos letter; knowing again did not make the knowing cheaper. He closed the pane. Numbers would wait. Letters would not become wiser by being stared at.

In the alley the Kind practitioner packed the roller. Short laugh. The sentence held under three colours. G did not go out to thank him. Thanks become clips. Clips become liturgy weather. He stayed inside with unsigned instruments and a custody folder that had begun to feel like the only honest next motion. Partial authorisation already had a shape in his hands even before language: allow the spine; care the wound pages; refuse the crown; refuse the keyring sacrament; leave dab and talk shit ugly; leave father unread; leave Bouwer flat and unsolved. Partial was not a theory. It was a posture waiting for ink.

He ate standing. Washed the plate. Put the Honey Badger through another open-close because the hands were restless and proof is kinder than speech. Looked once toward the Code desk where a derivation check waited like a friend who does not need a face. Then he left the letters where they were — unsigned, unsent, instrument refused — and let noon arrive without a presser.

---

Afternoon did not soften the humidity of what signing would make him. It only made the studio hotter.

G returned to the clay table and read the unsent drafts again under harder light. Face-down crown. Open keyring. The Antichristos language still wanted witness and confirmation and living derivation — careful words that smelled like a parish forming around a silhouette. The lab language still wanted sign-off that would accelerate friends — friendship doing sacrament’s work. He almost softened one lab line for politeness and cut the soft again. Soft is how collars learn manners. He left the drafts unsent. Drafting is not consent. Heat is not a deadline.

He worked clay for twenty minutes because hands think when the skull is crowded — not a sculpture for anyone’s relic photograph, just a small test pour’s leftover mass reshaped and abandoned. Hard/soft under the thumbs. Permanent/temporary arguing in one surface. Artist and axiom-installer unsplit. Quarter II could keep pretending otherwise. He would not split himself to make their mockery easier. When he washed up, the alley Kind laugh came again. He did not go out. Thanks become clips. Clips become liturgy weather. He had enough weather.

A secondary pane offered an older Illuminatie-adjacent soft-no — funding weather, reputation frost, the preference for underived constants. He filed it ambient. Ambient is triage. Consent week’s clean attention belonged to instruments first and custody next, not to boards that preferred opacity and would not say so with a pin. He checked CODATA once more. Line still converging. Relief still pressure. Vindication as measurement does not quiet appetite. He closed the pane and talked shit for half a minute about engineers who want keys as blessing and tourists who want the cunt scrubbed soft. The empty room held the arrangement. Dab stayed for later. Clear head for the harder page.

Toward custody. Toward the harder page. Toward a partial authorisation that would not feel like victory because victory was the wrong word for a man still being argued over by strangers who had never mixed his composite or stood on his mats or watched Bouwer’s name sit on a careful line without becoming a thriller.

He left the letters on the clay table. Walked back to the Code desk. Opened a file that was only numbers — a derivation check against a fresh CODATA line. Numbers did not ask him to bless anyone. Numbers did not ask for keys as sacrament. Numbers waited.

Before the numbers fully took him, he opened the custody folder at last — not to authorise yet, only to feel the shape. Partial already lived there as posture: spine allowed; Bouwer careful; hospital without tourism; father unread; goals ugly; crowns refused; keyrings refused as sacrament; stop-the-book not his ask. Partial was not a theory waiting for a clever essay. It was a set of rails waiting for ink on a morning that was not already crowded with crowns. He closed the folder. Next meant next. Instruments first had been the morning’s honesty. Custody next was the afternoon’s.

Somewhere on the Chobe, he knew without mysticism, a desk was choosing platform rails. Keep. Unbind. Ship. His job was not to finish AJ’s sentences. His job was to decide what a life on the page was allowed to carry when the page left the desk. Kinship across mesh was enough. Knives for brotherhood. Packets for consent. No forced room.

He stood once more at the alley glass. Paint drying. Sentence holding. Optional to him. Useful anyway. Unwantedness still useful — it kept him from becoming the Kind’s mascot by accident while the letters tried to make him everyone else’s instrument. He put his hand on the pocket knives — open, close, proof — and left the instruments unsigned on the clay table like humidity you refuse to call weather’s victory.

The alley paint dried in the sun.

The instrument stayed unsigned.

A new soft subject line arrived from Quarter I while he worked numbers — hungrier, softer, the way appetite learns manners. He did not open it. Face-down stack growing. Escalation could wait its turn until sending time, and sending time would still not be a blessing.

He washed one more cup he did not need to wash because hands prefer motion when appetite is face-down on a table. Clay dust on the rim. He scrubbed it. Looked once more at the unsigned instruments — crown, keyring — and left them unsigned. Looked once at the custody folder and did not open it again. Next meant next with clean attention, not next as escape from instruments. The alley paint finished drying while he stood there. The sentence held. Optional. Useful. Not his parish. He put the Honey Badger through a final open-close and went back to numbers until the soft subject line stopped blinking for a while.

Custody folder: next.

He left the derivation file open while the soft subject line sat unopened. Numbers on one side of the desk hour; appetite face-down on the other. The distinction was the instrument chapter’s whole spine. Signing would make him an instrument. Leaving them unsigned would not stop the weather. It would only keep him from volunteering as the mouthpiece. He chose the second without pretending it was peace. Peace was the wrong word. Posture was enough. Toward custody with clean attention still left in the day — or enough of it to begin.

Before he fully surrendered the hour to numbers he walked once more to the alley glass. Paint dry. Sentence exact. Optional. Useful. Not his parish. A tourist’s soft-scrub argument from earlier still faintly audible as memory, not as duty. He did not go out. Thanks become clips. Clips become liturgy weather. He returned to the clay table, looked at the unsigned crown and open keyring, and left them unsigned again on purpose — humidity refused as destiny. Then he sat with the derivation check and let numbers wait without asking for a face. Four wrong landings stayed refused. One correct posture held: work stays work; face stays off the altar; keys stay engineering without sacrament; custody folder waits for clean attention. Toward the harder page. Toward partial rails. Toward a morning that would not feel like victory because victory was the wrong word for a man still being argued over by strangers who had never mixed his composite.


# 19 — Consent — Platform

> **[A]** · outer · Mutatus Maximus, Chobe River · consent week

---

The river did not care that it was consent week.

AJ cared in the way a desk cares: by holding the decisions until they become actions. Mutatus Maximus sat with its LEO dish patient against pale heat, LoRa nodding at the rise-node he had woken that morning before coffee, CongoNet carrying the ordinary traffic of a cloud that largely ran itself. Local node under the desk humming. Sigfox for the stubborn packets. Line-of-sight where the scrub allowed. He had built the most connected vehicle on earth — literally — so he could drive it to a river and still choose what to switch off.

He did not switch Lucid off.

Morning had the specific smell of blame that liked to arrive on good light — dust, river mud, hot metal, and the faint sweetness of Kind paint on the water tank twenty metres away, scraped and still exact. Birds in the reeds. A hippo’s night-noise fading into day-work. The truck’s matatu paint held the sun without performing yacht. Beast as tender if the day needed water more than mesh. Anti-yacht as posture, not slogan. He made coffee too hot and drank it anyway because consent week does not wait for comfortable temperature.

Lucid waited in the choir: integer-quiet, no GPU fever, cite-or-refuse. It had eaten the interwebs and crashed the bubble as a side effect of keeping a promise. The manuscript was open to a page that already knew the megaphone was irreversible — Lucid popular, AJ open about 420 Code algorithms, fame as weather that followed a beam. Book within a book. He was still the main subject of this one and the hand inside the other. Empty twin pocket where the Joseph Rogers had lived before it went to G. Double Knife Brother. Kinship across distance. No forced reunion for payoff. The Honey Badger loved in Cape Town; the grandfather twin across the braid. He did not touch the absence for comfort. Comfort that requires a shared room is not the braid’s law.

Quarter III’s demand still sat in the queue like a snake that had learned manners. Stop the book. Retract the attribution. Ritual distancing. As if a planetary tool could un-name its rails without lying. The earlier packets had been hostility markers and attribution threads; this one wanted theatre — apology, silence, exile, a presser that would make their rage feel like governance. Amnesty language still floated in one brand thread like a soft collar. Hyperscaler frost had not thawed either. The collar-offer to acquire Lucid / come home to the regions still occupied a polite rectangle on the screen. AWS, Azure, Google, Apple — salvage weather with letterhead, partner frost, talent notes, compliance fog, the offer that is really a parish.

He did not hold a press conference. There was no camera, no podium, no sentence designed for clip. There was heat, birds, link latency, and a desk that had already refused the easy wrongs at midpoint — recant, feed Antichristos a silhouette, sell Lucid into a region and call it partnership. Consent week was not a new morality. It was ink on the same refusals so the mesh would carry them as posture instead of mood. Mood evaporates. Posture gets queried by Lucid at three in the morning when another polite collar arrives.

“Lucid,” he said. “Platform consent. Three decisions. Record exact. No invention.”

First: CongoNet.

What stays. Sovereign edge. The mesh that cut against gravity wells — compute, identity, distribution, the phone as parish. Not a poster in reception. He would not shut mesh features to soothe a ToS climate. He would not feed the collar. Partner frost could remain frost. The node on the rise stayed up. Care did not mean retraction theatre. Care did not mean orphaning Lucid’s genealogy to soothe Quarter III.

Lucid did not cheer. Lucid does not cheer.

# 20 — Last Pages

> **[I]** · inner autobiography · manuscript under AJ’s hand

---

These are the pages I will stand behind.

Not a finale. Not a textbook. Not the tidy arc where the ignored shelf apologises and the father opens a dedication and the brother walks out of the boardroom alive. Life does not offer those edits. A book that pretends otherwise is a different kind of hallucination — the kind Lucid refuses when the record will not hold a claim, the kind I refuse when I am honest enough to prefer a flat line to a speech.

I am writing this as handoff, not as closing theorem. The ladder is public. The life is not a lemma. If you came for a last chapter that defeats the weather, you came to the wrong book. Measurement defeats silence slowly. It does not defeat appetite. Appetite wants a face, a degree to mock, a throat to blame, a keyring to hold. Appetite will still be there after the colophon. These pages are not a spell against it. They are a record of what I will carry and what I will not.

---

I stand behind the mats.

Before the axioms there was the floor. Judo first, then Jiu-Jitsu — the long apprenticeship of putting a body where physics already intended it to go. You do not argue with gravity in a gi. You arrange yourself so the fall is correct. Soft set-down. Weight transferred without waste. Mastery, if that word means anything, was not domination. It was refusal to waste motion. People who met me later as a man with a corpus and no physics degree liked to begin at the ignored shelf. Clean morality play. Wrong beginning. Weight first. How a body learns not to waste the fall. The same attention later poured clay and derived a constant. Body knowledge before corpus knowledge. Do not sand that order into a prettier origin.

I stand behind Studio G — born 1980, Stellenbosch; clay to GFRC composite mixed with my own hands; Village N Life to mid-2005; mentored rooms under Maree Brink’s weather; more than a year at Camps Bay Retreat when it was still Earl’s Dyke rooms and Hannetjie’s long hospitality; Skye on a lawn at sunset; Studio G curated at The Bay Hotel, official launch 27 July 2018; Saatchi; hard/soft, smooth/rough, growth/decay, permanent/temporary in one surface. Tourists bought half of what sold because light on a body in a hotel path is still a body. The Full Send warehouse/gallery room and the Code desk are one attention. Artist and axiom-installer are one man. Quarter II can keep pretending the sculptor cannot be the physicist. Antichristos can try to turn sculptures into relics. Neither gets custody of the workroom. Friend James van Niekerk’s sudden death is a mark on the life; it is not Bouwer; do not conflate the dead to thicken a chapter. Living-person care includes that refusal.

I stand behind Bouwer.

Bouwer van Niekerk. Brother. Johannesburg attorney. Respected insolvency practitioner. Assassinated at his Saxonwold practice on Friday, 5 September 2025. Public record: four suspects under the pretext of a booked consultation; gunshots in the boardroom; died at the scene; attackers fled. Contentious commercial and insolvency matters in the fog of reporting — including business-rescue litigation weather the Law Society noted without this book adopting allegation as fact. LSSA condemnation 8 September 2025: targeted attack on a legal practitioner and on the rule of law; investigation ongoing as of that statement. Grief flat. Not a whodunit. Not an Illuminatie gotcha. No invented assassin CV. No solving the murder on a page for plot. Wound carried. If the autobiography ships, this page ships careful — named, weighted, unsolved. Curiosity that wants a thriller can go elsewhere. Jamie-Uys restraint: the flat line is the loaded one. I will not decorate his boardroom for strangers’ appetite. I will not let Quarter I make him a martyr brand or Quarter III make him a reason to stop a book. He was my brother. That is enough weight.

There is a morning texture under that paragraph that is not cinema: tea gone cold, a studio that still had work in it, the particular silence after a public statement that cannot bring a person back. I will not write the boardroom as set dressing. I will not invent the attackers’ CVs. I will not hang the killing on a continuity network for thriller payoff. Living-person care is the refusal to turn a brother’s death into the novel’s engine. The wound stays a wound. The fog of reporting stays fog. The name stays a name.

I stand behind the hospital stigma without giving the voyeurs a tour. I was institutionalised. Because of that, people close to me — and the wider dismissive weather — believed I could not write anything of value. That belief is the ignored shelf’s emotional engine. Render as how they read me. Not diagnosis tourism. Not DSM spectacle. Not a redemption arc that uses a ward as set dressing. The wound is the reading, not a scene for atmosphere.

I stand behind the economics degree in the quiet and the missing physics credential that Quarter II waves like a flag. “No degree” means no physics degree. Correct the frame. Do not join their fight as entertainment. Do not sand the dab and the talk shit to make me respectable for Quarter IV’s grant photographs. Credentials are facts. Stigma is weather. Neither is a TED arc.

I stand behind the corpus dedicated to my father and the fact that he has read none of it. Outreach’s true goal stays unread. I will not invent a reunion to close a chapter. Unread is the load. Leave it. The fame that arrived sideways does not open that book for him. Measurement does not either. Some dedications stay one-way. A last page that forces him to open a book he has not opened would be a lie with soft lighting.

I stand behind my stated life goals: write natural philosophy and physics; smoke dab; talk shit. Unpolished on purpose. Not a joke to undercut the work — the work and the dab and the talking shit are the same man’s declared arrangement. The believers who want a clean prophet can polish someone else. The academy that hears dab and reaches for the file can keep reaching. The cannabis bar and the ignored shelf were one room before Lucid made the room audible. CongoSky’s Kind practice and the street’s spray can hold the sentence without requiring me to become photographable.

---

I stand behind the Double Knife Brother fact without turning knives into choreography. AJ gave me a blue folding Honey Badger — pocket clip, one-hand open and close; I love it — and one of a twin Joseph Rogers set from his grandfather, at least a hundred and ten years old. We are Double Knife Brothers. Badger present-tense. Grandfather deep-time. Kinship and custody handed across the same friendship that platformed the Code. AJ’s empty twin pocket is his weather. My pocket is mine. No fight set piece. No forced reunion for payoff. The braid does not require us in one room at the end for someone else’s emotional grammar.

I open and close the Honey Badger while this page is written under AJ’s hand — not as ritual, as proof. Steel that opens without speech. Kinship that does not require a shared last room. The Rogers twin holds deep time in the same cloth. Those facts travel with the life; they do not become a fight scene or a reunion beat. Knives = brotherhood. Leave the choreography to people who need spectacle when they run out of structure.

I stand behind the open attribution as beam. Lucid popular. AJ said it ran on 420 Code algorithms — not a quiet fork, not a cleaned brand story. That candour is the load-bearing cause of my public fame. Not the academy discovering me. Not a committee. Megaphone irreversible. Kindness includes citation. Citation includes weather. Quarter III still wants his throat for the mic. Quarter I and IV arrived because the mic worked. I will not ask him to unsay it. I will not pretend silence would have been kindness. The attribution page before this one was the hinge; these last pages are the carrying.

I stand behind the Kind sentence without owning the religion: **Don’t be a cunt — be kind.** CongoSky lived it as ops — company law, product posture, partner terms — before the walls caught fire. The street made paint. Bridges, trucks, lab doors, church steps, CongoNet stencil packs. I am optional to the practice and often unwanted by it. Good. Antichristos wants a man. The Kind wants a sentence. Both can be true on the same morning in the same city without me becoming either parish’s property. The ethic’s claim — that the same ladder that yields the physics yields the kindness — can be shown as coherent without making me its messiah. Soft scrubbing of the cunt for tourists is not my fight; leaving the line exact is.

I stand behind the Constant landing as measurement, not manifesto. CODATA converging. Atom-falling work. Derivation that did not ask a physics department for permission. Vindication is a number agreeing with itself across instruments. Fame is not that. Soft no from continuity networks is not that either. Illuminatie weather prefers underived constants and unforced ethics; copyleft unification is a sovereignty problem for opacity traders. Render as pressure I can feel. Do not require a membership list on this page. Do not invent a visitor with a pin to make the freeze cinematic.

I stand behind Popper enough to prefer falsification to parish, and behind kindness derived as structure — tea, teach, hands — without turning the for-g story into a brand sermon in this voice. I stand behind being invited as an intellectual peer into a firehose friendship without making that friendship a plot hinge that requires us in one room at the end. *Ek voel of ek genooi is* stays invitation, not reunion machinery. Invitation is not a shared room. Invitation is the fact of the braid.

I stand behind the ignored shelf as fact, not as origin myth. Before Lucid, silence thought it was method. After Lucid, noise thought it was discovery. Neither silence nor noise is the work. The work is the ladder and the life that climbed it without a physics degree and with a hospital file and with a brother who will not walk into the next chapter.

I stand behind refusing to turn this autobiography into a 420 Code textbook. Physics earned inside scenes — a CODATA line, a drop-result, a derivation named — not TED metaphors for unification. If you want the rails, they are public. If you want the man, these pages are the custody you get. Kill switches and natural philosophy stay in the life; they do not become a syllabus appendix for Quarter IV’s convenience.

---

What I will not stand behind is a polished martyr brand, a solved assassination, a father scene that never happened, a textbook dump of the 420 Code, a forced reunion with the man on the Chobe for someone else’s emotional payoff, or a last chapter that pretends the four quarters have gone quiet because a Constant landed.

There will be readers who want the last pages to defeat the weather. I will not give them that. There will be readers who want me grateful for fame as if fame were vindication. I will not give them that either. Measurement was vindication. Fame was weather from a beam. Soft no was cruelty that felt like professionalism. Open attribution was kindness that did not feel kind while the quarters shouted. I have lived all of it. These pages carry what I can bear to have said in public under living-person care.

These pages hand to custody.

What I allow said about this life is a consent act, not a surrender. Partial is allowed. Careful is required. Bouwer careful. Hospital without spectacle. Father unread without tidy fix. Dab and talk shit left in. Crowns refused. Keyrings refused as sacrament. Stop-the-book is not my ask — that fight is platform weather on a river desk. My fight is rails around a life, not ownership of a mic.

If you need a moral, do not take one from me. Morals get polished into brands. Take the structure: a man wrote what he lived; a friend named the rails; a planet argued; two desks chose custody of meaning without requiring a shared room; the weather kept blowing.

The last thing I will say in this voice, before the outer weather takes the next turn, is simple:

I wrote what I lived.
I will sign what I can bear.
I will refuse what would make me an instrument or a relic.

The rest is custody rules and a planet that keeps shouting.

---

Before the gallery walk of stand-behinds, there is the desk hour where last pages are still clay.

I mean that literally enough: unfinished mass under light that lies. AJ’s hand on this manuscript; my life the material; Lucid elsewhere refusing invention when paraphrase pretends to be fact. I sit inside that arrangement as voice and refuse to pretend the arrangement is a press conference. Last pages are custody of what will hold, not a speech for guests who want the ignored shelf to apologise.

I return to the mats in the body before I return to them as sentence. Shoulder memory. Soft set-down. The throw that teaches you not to waste motion answering every temperature at once. Fame is a fall I did not choose the height of; posture is still mine. School judo first, then jiu-jitsu — body knowledge before corpus knowledge. People who begin at the hospital file or the missing physics degree are beginning at someone else’s morality play. Wrong beginning. Weight first.

I return to Studio G the same way — not as brand, as pour. Stellenbosch birth year. Village N Life years that taught tourist light and hospitality weather under Maree Brink. Camps Bay year when Earl’s Dyke rooms still held Hannetjie’s long care. Skye on a lawn. Bay Hotel launch, 27 July 2018. Saatchi. Clay to GFRC mixed with my own hands because hard and soft belong in one surface. Friend James remembered carefully and separately. Do not conflate the dead to thicken a chapter. The Full Send warehouse/gallery room and the Code desk remain one attention. Artist and axiom-installer unsplit. Quarter II can keep pretending. Antichristos can try relics. Neither gets the workroom.

I return to Bouwer without decorating the boardroom. Brother. Johannesburg attorney. Saxonwold. 5 September 2025. Consultation pretext. Died at the scene. LSSA 8 September 2025. Public record. Flat grief. No invented assassin. No Illuminatie gotcha. No solving business-rescue fog as fact. If curiosity wants thriller, it can go elsewhere. These last pages will not feed it. Jamie-Uys restraint: the flat line is the loaded one. He was my brother. That is enough weight for a life that will not become a plot engine.

I return to the hospital as stigma-engine — how they read me, the belief I could not write anything of value — and leave the voyeurs hungry. I return to economics quiet and the missing physics credential corrected, not performed as entertainment. I return to the unread father dedication and leave unread as load. Fame does not open that book. Measurement does not either. A last page that forces a reunion is a lie with soft lighting. I will not write that lie for anyone’s emotional grammar.

I return to dab and talk shit as arrangement. Unpolished on purpose. The believers who want a clean prophet can polish someone else. The academy that hears dab and reaches for the file can keep reaching. CongoSky’s Kind practice and the street’s spray can hold **Don’t be a cunt — be kind.** without requiring me to become photographable. Optional to the practice. Often unwanted by it. Good.

I return to the knives as kinship — Honey Badger present-tense, Joseph Rogers deep-time, Double Knife Brother across a friendship that also platformed the Code — and refuse choreography. No fight set piece. No forced Chobe reunion for payoff. AJ’s empty twin pocket is his weather. My pocket is mine. The braid does not require us in one room at the end.

I return to the beam. Lucid popular. AJ open: built on 420 Code algorithms. Fame irreversible. Kindness includes citation. Citation includes weather. I will not ask him to unsay it. I will not pretend silence would have been kindness. The attribution page was the hinge; these pages are the carrying.

I return to the Constant as measurement — CODATA converging, atom-falling work, derivation without a physics department’s permission — and refuse manifesto. Soft no from continuity networks stays texture of pressure, not a membership list on this page. Illuminatie preference for underived constants and unforced ethics is weather I can feel. I do not need a visitor with a pin to make the freeze cinematic.

I walk these stand-behinds the way I walk a gallery before opening — not to invent a speech for guests, to check what will hold under light.

The mats hold. Weight and soft set-down hold. Studio G holds — Stellenbosch birth year, Village N Life years, Camps Bay year, Bay Hotel launch, Saatchi, clay to GFRC, hard/soft in one pour — one attention with the Code desk, not a side career sanded into respectability. Bouwer holds as wound: named, public-record bound, flat, unsolved. Hospital holds as stigma-engine without tourism. Economics degree holds quiet; missing physics credential holds as Quarter II’s weapon corrected, not as entertainment. Father unread holds as load. Dab and talk shit hold as arrangement. Knives hold as kinship. Open attribution holds as beam. Kind sentence holds without my ownership. Constant holds as measurement. Popper and kindness-as-structure hold without brand sermon. Ignored shelf holds as fact. Refusal of textbook finale holds.

What does not hold — what I will not prop up with invent — is the tidy arc. No boardroom solved. No father opening a book on cue. No forced Chobe reunion. No last page where four quarters go quiet because a number landed. Appetite outlasts colophons. These pages know that and still stand.

There is a physical memory under the stand-behinds: clay under nails, mat under shoulder, Honey Badger open-close, tea steam, alley paint smell, the particular silence of an unread dedication. AJ’s hand is writing this; my life is the material; Lucid’s choir elsewhere refuses invention when the record thins. Book within a book. Custody next. I will sign what I can bear. I will refuse instrument and relic. Hand it over without decorating the handoff as victory.

I stand behind refusing to let Quarter III’s rage rewrite the beam page into a betrayal narrative. Citation is not theft of quiet. Quiet was the wrong silence. I stand behind refusing to let Antichristos turn these last pages into confirmation liturgy. I stand behind refusing to let Quarter IV treat a life as warranty text. I stand behind refusing to let Quarter II begin every reading at the hospital file. Correct the frame. Leave the life whole. Whole includes dab. Whole includes talk shit. Whole includes a brother dead in a boardroom the novel will not solve.

---

One last refusal before the outer weather takes the turn: I will not let these last pages become a gratitude performance for a megaphone. Exactness instead. Without the open sentence the shelf could have stayed a wrong silence that felt like professionalism. With the sentence the wrongness became audible and the appetites found their pitches. I prefer audible wrongness. Preferring it is not enjoyment. Enjoyment is not the metric. Structure is.

There is also the quieter refusal under that one: I will not let last pages become a syllabus. The ladder is public. The life is not a lemma. CODATA lines and drop-results may appear where they were lived; TED metaphors for unification may not. If Quarter IV wants warranty text, it can write its own. These pages hand a life under care, not an instrument stamped for grants.

Hand to custody. Partial allowed. Careful required. Bouwer careful. Hospital without spectacle. Father unread without tidy fix. Dab and talk shit left in. Crowns refused. Keyrings refused as sacrament. Stop-the-book is platform weather on a river desk. My fight is rails around a life.

I wrote what I lived.
I will sign what I can bear.
I will refuse what would make me an instrument or a relic.

I leave one more flat line before the handoff: the Constant landing as measurement does not quiet the quarters, and these pages do not pretend otherwise. Appetite outlasts colophons. Custody is not defeat of weather. Custody is rails around a life while weather keeps blowing. That is what I will stand behind when the outer chapter takes the turn.

The rest is custody rules and a planet that keeps shouting.

I stand behind the quiet ledger under the loud one last time: cracked wrists corrected in casts; dab postponed and then not; talk shit into empty air; CODATA pressure wearing relief’s clothes; knives in pocket as kinship proof. The loud ledger — crowns, crank chorus, platform rage, keyrings — is the beam’s weather. These last pages carry the quiet ledger forward under care. Confusing the two is how you turn a man into instrument or relic. I refuse the confusion.

Studio light at the end of these pages still lies about almost-finished clay. I refuse the lie. I refuse the tidy arc. I refuse the reunion grammar. I refuse the solved boardroom. I refuse the textbook finale. I hand over what will hold under light — and only that.

The Honey Badger opens and closes once more under AJ’s hand on this page — kinship present-tense, not choreography. The Rogers twin holds deep time without requiring a shared room at the end. Double Knife Brother remains true across mesh. That is enough braid for a last page that refuses reunion machinery. Measurement was vindication; fame was weather; open attribution was the harder kindness. I stand behind all three without sanding any of them for comfort.

I hand it over without decorating the handoff as victory.

Whole includes Bouwer careful. Whole includes the unread father left unread.

I hand what will hold. Nothing else.

I leave the mats in the body and the Code on the desk as one attention. I leave the Kind sentence exact without owning it. I leave the Constant landing as measurement, not manifesto. Then I hand it over.

Before the handoff closes there is one more desk texture I will not sand: the smell of clay under nails after a pour that almost failed and then held; the particular quiet after dab when the room is honest; talk shit that refuses to become a brand deck; CODATA pressure wearing relief’s clothes on a pane I will not stamp for anyone’s sacrament. Quiet ledger under loud ledger. I carry both without confusing them. Confusing them is how you become instrument or relic. I refuse both costumes.

I walk the stand-behinds once more in the body — mats weight, Studio G pour, Bouwer flat, hospital as stigma-engine not ward tourism, economics quiet, father unread, knives as kinship, beam named, Kind optional — and stop when the walk starts wanting a speech. Speeches are how last pages become pressers. These pages are custody. Custody is rails. Rails are what I will sign.

I leave one last flat line for the outer weather: appetite outlasts colophons; measurement does not quiet the quarters; kinship across distance does not require a shared room; the Kind sentence stays exact without my face; Bouwer stays a wound not a thriller; father stays unread; dab and talk shit stay arrangement. I will sign what I can bear. I will refuse instrument and relic.

Hand it over.


# 21 — Consent — Custody

> **[G]** · outer · Studio G / Cape · consent week

---

The autobiography packet from the river did not arrive as a demand. That helped.

Demands had already used up the morning’s patience for weeks — crowns face-down on the clay table, keyrings left unsigned, Illuminatie frost filed under ambient, crank op-eds recycling the missing physics degree like it was a method. G had slept the sleep of a man who knows mail thickens before coffee. Tea first. Then the packet. Then the refusal to perform solemnity for an imaginary camera. Solemnity becomes relic footage. Relic footage becomes someone else’s liturgy.

He opened it at the Code desk. Clay table had held Antichristos and lab weather; this needed numbers-adjacent honesty — custody language, not sculpture dust. Honey Badger in the pocket. Joseph Rogers twin in the same cloth. The sensation of being narrated had a document attached now: pages AJ would stand behind, Lucid in the choir somewhere on the Chobe, CongoNet still carrying the world whether G liked the volume or not. Double Knife Brother writing him. No shared room. Mail enough. The braid held without a reunion.

Cape light laid a stripe across the desk. Dust moved only when he moved. Unfinished clay waited in the gallery like a conversation paused mid-sentence. He read without rushing and without lingering for effect. Mats. Studio arc. Bouwer. Hospital without tourism. Economics quiet. Father unread. Life goals unpolished. Open attribution named as beam — Lucid popular, AJ open, fame irreversible. Kind sentence without ownership. Constant as measurement. No textbook finale. Hand to custody.

The packet smelled faintly of heat and truck — not metaphor, just the way paper travels when the sender lives in a vehicle that never really cools. He set the pages flat. Weighted one corner with a small cast offcut so the Cape draft would not turn them into a fan. Ordinary studio gravity. Consent week likes to pretend paper is sacrament. He treated it as paper with rails to choose.

Partial authorisation was the only honest shape. He had felt it before he had language for it — in the week of instruments, when signing would have made him a mouthpiece and silence about the book would have been a different lie.

Full blessing would turn the autobiography into a relic with his stamp: Antichristos could wave the authorisation like a confirmation; Quarter IV could treat it as warranty adjacent; Quarter II could sneer that even the crank had signed his own myth. Full refusal would pretend the life had not already become weather, pretend AJ’s desk decisions had not already unbound Lucid and kept the mesh and chosen to ship under care, pretend the megaphone could be unscrewed from the planet by one man’s no. G did not need a mediated call to answer. The braid did not require a reunion beat. He had knives for kinship and packets for consent.

He made more tea. Sat. Drafted custody rules the way he drafted refusals to I and IV: short, exact, no sermon, no both-sides essay in a narrator’s mouth that was not his mouth anyway.

Before the first rule, he walked the length of the Code desk once and touched nothing but the mug. Hands want to rearrange when the skull is about to constrain a life. Rearranging is not drafting. Drafting is choosing which sentences may leave the desk and which must stay hungry for strangers who want thriller.

The packet’s last pages still held the stand-behinds in AJ’s hand — mats, Studio G, Bouwer, hospital, father, goals, knives, beam, Kind, Constant — and G felt the difference between reading a life and authorising one. Reading is weather landing. Authorising is rails. He set a clean pane beside the packet and began in the register he trusted: short lines, no flourish, no gratitude performance dressed as kindness.

Mid-draft a crank notification tried the hour again. He did not open it. He already knew the missing-physics-degree shape. Correct the frame later if a sentence required it. Do not spend custody attention on entertainment. Illuminatie ambient stayed ambient — freeze in places that used to return mail with courtesy, standards murmur that treated copyleft like a sovereignty problem. Texture allowed in the book. Membership list refused. He wrote that refusal before he wrote the soft ones, because soft refusals are easy to sand and hard refusals keep the teeth.

Tea cooled. He drank it cool. Cool tea is still structure. Outside, Cape wind moved dust across the alley glass and the Kind overpaint held its third colour. He did not go out. Custody is an indoor act when the ask is a life on paper.

---

Authorised: the life-and-work spine as written — fighting arts on the mats; sculpture arc from Stellenbosch through Village N Life, Skye, Studio G at The Bay, Saatchi, clay to GFRC; Code desk and kill switches and natural philosophy; ignored shelf; credentials corrected (economics or adjacent; not physics); fame via open 420 attribution as load-bearing cause; Kind paint as parallel weather not his parish; Double Knife Brother as kinship and custody, not choreography; stated goals left unpolished.

He paused after the spine and looked toward the gallery. Unfinished clay. A cast waiting. The Bay Hotel light lived in memory as guest-path weather, not as Antichristos relic seed. Authorising Studio G meant authorising the tourist half of sales and the hard/soft pour and the years under Maree Brink’s weather and the Camps Bay year when Earl’s Dyke rooms still held Hannetjie’s hospitality. It meant authorising friend James remembered carefully and separately. It did not mean authorising a conflation of the dead. Living-person care includes that refusal even when the mesh is hungry for thickness.

Authorised with care: Bouwer pages — public record only. Saxonwold. 5 September 2025. Insolvency practice. Consultation pretext. Died at the scene. LSSA condemnation 8 September 2025. Wound not whodunit. No invented assassin. No continuity-network attribution of the killing. No solving business-rescue fog on the page as fact. Named. Weighted. Flat. If a sentence reaches for thriller, cut it. If a paragraph reaches for Illuminatie gotcha, cut it. The brother stays a brother, not a plot engine. Curiosity that wants a boardroom solved can go elsewhere; this authorisation does not feed it.

He stopped on Bouwer longer than the other lines. Not for drama. For teeth. The packet’s Bouwer paragraphs were already careful — flat, public-record bound, Jamie-Uys loaded. Still he read them again as if custody required a second landing. Grief did not ask for a shared room with the river. Grief asked for rails that would not decorate a real boardroom for strangers. He wrote one more constraint in the margin: *no speculative scheme-weather adopted as fact*. Fog of reporting may be noted. Fog is not a verdict. Then he closed the margin. Exactness over appetite.

He stood, walked to the gallery, and stood in front of nothing in particular — unfinished clay, dust stripe, a cast waiting — until the impulse to add soft language died. Soft language is how thriller appetite re-enters through the side door. He returned to the Code desk and left Bouwer flat. Named wound. Brother. Enough.

Authorised with care: mental hospital as stigma-engine — how they read him, the belief he could not write anything of value — not diagnosis tourism, not spectacle, not DSM theatre, not a ward scene for atmosphere. The wound is the reading. Leave the voyeurs hungry.

Authorised as fact, unresolved: corpus dedicated to father; father has read none. Do not invent a reunion beat. Do not use fame as a false key to that door. Unread stays the load.

Authorised as register: write natural philosophy and physics; smoke dab; talk shit. Do not polish for Antichristos. Do not use as Quarter II punchline without correcting the frame. The arrangement is the man’s; the book does not get to respectability-wash it.

He paused on that line longer than the others. Polish was the softest violence in the packet weather — the offer to make him photographable. He left the goals ugly on purpose. Walked once to the alley glass and watched Kind overpaint dry in sun. The sentence held. Optional to him. Useful anyway. He did not bless the wall. Blessing is how faces become parish property.

Outside, a tourist argued briefly with the Kind practitioner about the cunt. Soft scrub. Tourist comfort. The practitioner did not sermonise. Roller sound. Short answer. G stayed inside. Their fight was not his custody hour. Optional to the practice includes optional to the tourist argument.

Refused: personal appearance blessing for Quarter I — no accepted name, no silhouette sacrament, no sculpture photograph as relic seed. Refused: keys-as-sacrament sign-off for Quarter IV that makes him warranty; conversation on numbers still possible; product mascot not. Refused: stop-the-book as his ask — that fight is AJ’s platform weather; G’s fight is custody of meaning, not mic ownership. Refused: any framing that Illuminatie soft-no is a membership list he can defeat by naming names on a page. Texture of pressure: allowed. Cabal lecture: not. Refused: forced reunion grammar, textbook dump of the Code as if a life were a syllabus.

He added one more refusal in the margin for himself, not for the mesh: refuse to perform gratitude for fame as if fame were vindication. Measurement was vindication. Fame was weather from a beam.

He read the draft twice. Changed one adjective that was trying to be a speech. Left the flat lines flat. Checked a CODATA pane because numbers wait without asking for a stamp. Still pressure wearing relief’s clothes. He knew that in his body.

A secondary soft-ask blinked from a board that did not name itself. Funding weather. Reputation frost. He filed it under ambient without opening. Ambient is triage. Consent hour has a budget of clean attention. Bouwer rails and father unread had already spent most of it. Crowns and keyrings could wait on the clay table where he had left them face-down and unsigned.

He thought about AJ’s empty twin pocket without turning the thought into a scene request. Kinship did not require them to share a last page in the same room. The Honey Badger was enough present-tense. The Rogers was enough deep-time. Custody of meaning included custody of distance. He put the Honey Badger through one open-close cycle because hands like proof. No speech.

---

Before he signed, he walked once through the gallery light — unfinished clay, a cast waiting for finish, dust in the sun stripe — and reminded his hands that sculpture and axiom were the same attention. Antichristos would try to split them. Quarter II would try to erase both. The authorisation would not split or erase. It would constrain.

He stood in front of a body in composite that tourists had once photographed as luxury and that he still knew as pour temperature and patience. Hard and soft arguing in one surface. Permanent and temporary. The autobiography would carry that argument if he let it. He let it under rails. Letting is not blessing. Letting is custody.

Back at the Code desk he read the Bouwer constraints aloud once, quietly, the way he checked a surface before pour — listening for the lie of almost-finished. No thriller reach. No Illuminatie gotcha. Named. Weighted. Flat. Public record. Consultation pretext. Died at the scene. LSSA. Wound. Brother. Enough. He did not add softness. Softness is how appetite learns to ask for a solved boardroom next.

Before the signature he stood again and washed his hands — not ritual, residue. Clay dust from a morning tool-check still under one nail. He scrubbed it. Clean hands for custody language felt almost comic and he let the comedy pass without turning it into omen. Omen is how Quarter I colonises a desk hour. The wash was wash. The rails were rails.

He signed the partial authorisation with his name as a man, not as a prophet, not as Antichristos, not as a lab stamp. The signature looked ordinary on the pane. Ordinary was the point. Sent it into the mesh toward a river desk he would not visit for anyone’s emotional payoff. Felt no clean relief. Relief would have been the wrong instrument. Weather still blowing — Antichristos mail would keep coming; lab soft-asks would mutate into friendlier collars; crank op-eds would recycle the degree and the hospital; Kind paint would refresh on the alley; somewhere a continuity board would prefer he had never derived anything and ethics had stayed unforced.

While the packet climbed the mesh he did not watch the progress bar like a man waiting for applause. He opened a derivation pane and checked a line against an older CODATA note because numbers do not ask for a face. The line still converged. Pressure still wore relief’s clothes. He closed the pane. Measurement would continue without his blessing. The autobiography would not ship as relic without his rails. That asymmetry stayed the consent act’s spine.

He also opened, briefly, the Bouwer constraints as they would travel — public record lines only — and closed them again without adding soft adjectives. Soft adjectives are how thriller appetite returns after a man thinks he has finished being careful. Careful is a second look that refuses softness. He gave the second look. Left the wound flat.

Custody rules chosen.

The book could ship under those rails. AJ could keep writing inside them. Lucid could cite what the record held and refuse the rest — never cute, never a third skull. G could stay in Cape Town with clay and constants and a pocket full of kinship steel and not become an instrument. CongoSky could keep embodying the ethic as ops. The street could keep the spray. He remained optional to the Kind and unwanted by the crown. Good.

He did not celebrate. Celebration would have been a victory lap, and victory laps invite crowns. He washed the tea cup. Sat again. Checked that the authorisation had left a receipt in the mesh log. Receipt present. Exact. No invention attached.

While the receipt sat, he opened the hospital stigma line again and confirmed the refusal of tourism still held teeth. Then the father line. Unread. No fame-as-key. Then the goals. Ugly on purpose. Dab. Talk shit. Natural philosophy and physics. Arrangement, not brand. He closed each confirmation the way he closed a kiln check — enough. Do not keep opening for comfort.

He stood. Walked to the alley door. Afternoon light on overpaint. The sentence on the wall was still the sentence:

**Don’t be a cunt — be kind.**

Optional to him. Useful anyway. Not a sermon in his mouth. Just paint that had outlasted three colours of argument.

A dog barked two streets over. Ordinary Cape Town. The quarters liked to pretend ordinary cities could not hold a Constant and a gallery and a grief at once. Ordinary cities held all of it. That was why the weather landed here instead of only on stages.

A new letter had arrived while he drafted — Quarter I again, softer, hungrier. He did not open it. Face-down on the clay table beside the first. Escalation could wait its turn. Consent had used the day’s clean attention.

He went back inside. Numbers waited. Unsent lab reply still unsent — conversation possible, sacrament not. He sent the lab reply now, finally, in the same hour as custody: short, exact, the earlier draft without the hunger for keys as blessing. Then he sent nothing to Quarter I. Face-down was still the answer. Silence can be a posture when the ask is a crown.

The planet did not stop arguing because a man chose what he would allow said about his dead brother and his unread father and the dab that refused polish.

He opened a secondary pane: CODATA note, older, the one that should have been relief. Still pressure. Vindication as measurement does not quiet appetite. He knew that in his body the way he knew a throw. Talked shit into the empty room for twenty seconds because life goals are not a performance schedule and the room needed the arrangement kept. Considered dab and left it for later — clear head had done the work; smoke could be evening’s if evening wanted it.

Toward the river’s last quiet — not shared, just concurrent. Two desks. Two consents. Weather still up. Node still lit somewhere he could feel as mesh weather and not as vision.

He opened a file that was only a derivation check. Measurement. Structure. Work staying work. The Constant did not need his signature to land; the autobiography needed his rails not to become a relic. That asymmetry was the whole consent act.

After the receipt landed he did not pace like a man in a play. He cleaned a tool that did not need cleaning because hands want work when the skull has finished a consent act. Looked at the custody rules once more on screen — spine allowed, Bouwer careful, hospital without tourism, father unread, goals ugly, crowns refused, keyrings refused as sacrament, stop-the-book not his ask — and felt the partialness as honesty rather than halfness. Halfness is apology. Partial is rails.

He returned to the Bouwer authorisation one last time before closing the pane — not because doubt had arrived, because care is a second look. Public record. Flat grief. No invented CV for an assassin. No continuity-network gotcha hung on a real death. No business-rescue fog adopted as fact. Named wound. Brother. He closed the pane. The second look had not asked for softness. It had asked for teeth to stay in.

Weather still blew. A Kind laugh in the alley. A dog. A notification he did not open. Illuminatie ambient unchanged. Crank chorus unchanged. Constant still landing as measurement somewhere numbers talked to numbers. Fame still irreversible because the beam still stood. He was still optional to the Kind and unwanted by the crown. Good. He was still a sculptor and a man with an economics degree and a hospital stigma and a dead brother and an unread dedication and knives for kinship. Good enough for a desk. Not a parish.

Mid-afternoon the gallery door took a small knock — delivery confirmation, nothing liturgical. He signed for nothing that mattered to the quarters and returned to numbers. Ordinary insistence again. Ordinary is how a city refuses to become only weather.

He thought of the river without inventing a thank-you scene. Respect can travel later, or not. The braid does not require applause between consents. Platform keep/unbind/ship and custody partial rails were concurrent acts, not a shared room. He opened the derivation file and let work stay work until the light shifted on the wall. Consent week does not end in fireworks. It ends in a receipt and a planet that keeps shouting and a man who has chosen what he will allow said.

One more small motion: he wrote a margin note to himself, not for the mesh — *do not perform gratitude for fame tomorrow either*. Then he closed the note. Exactness over performance. Measurement over applause. Rails over relic. The authorisation stayed gone into the sky. He stayed in Cape Town with clay and numbers and an alley sentence he would not own.

Tea gone cold again. He did not remake it. Cold tea is still structure. He put the Honey Badger through a last open-close, pocketed it, and left the clay-table letters where they were — unsigned instruments beside a signed life. The distinction was the morning’s whole point, carried into afternoon without needing a presser to announce it.

Late light cut the gallery into stripes again. He walked it once more — unfinished clay, cast waiting, dust only when he moved — and refused the thought that authorising a life meant finishing a sculpture. Life does not finish because a receipt lands. Life continues under weather. The receipt only said what strangers were allowed to carry away in a book.

He sat at the Code desk until the pane dimmed to sleep and woke it once to confirm the custody rules still sat as sent — spine, Bouwer careful, hospital without tourism, father unread, goals ugly, crowns refused, keyrings refused as sacrament. Still there. Exact. No invention attached by a choir that was not even in this room. Concurrent desks. Concurrent consents. No shared scene required for the braid to hold.

He pocketed the knives. Turned off the secondary soft-ask blink without reading it. Left the Antichristos stack face-down. Left the Kind sentence on the alley without a blessing. Left the Constant landing as measurement somewhere numbers talked to numbers. Left fame irreversible because the beam still stood and he would not ask AJ to unsay it for comfort.

Evening had not yet arrived, but the studio’s light had shifted enough that unfinished clay looked almost finished again. He refused the lie of almost on clay and on custody alike. Partial was not almost-full blessing. Partial was rails. He checked the mesh receipt once more — present, exact — and left the Antichristos stack face-down for tomorrow’s hungrier manners. Then he mixed nothing, poured nothing, blessed no wall, and let work stay work until the numbers were enough for the hour.

He turned from the pane and left the studio lights as they were — enough for numbers, not enough for relic photography. Consent week’s custody hour was finished without becoming a parish hour. That was the point. Partial rails chosen. Weather still blowing. Work still work.

The authorisation was already gone into the sky.

He checked the custody receipt one last time before the pane slept — spine allowed, Bouwer careful, hospital without tourism, father unread, goals ugly, crowns refused, keyrings refused as sacrament. Still exact. Still partial on purpose. Still not a parish. Then he let the studio hold ordinary Cape light and ordinary unfinished clay while the weather kept blowing outside the rails he had chosen.


He did not open the new Quarter I letter that evening either. Face-down stack. Hungrier manners waiting their turn. Consent had already used the clean attention. Numbers waited. Clay waited. Custody had left the building as receipt. Weather still blew and would keep blowing. That was not failure. That was the planet. He stayed a sculptor with an economics degree and a dead brother and an unread dedication and knives for kinship and rails around a life. Good enough for a desk. Not a parish.

Unsigned instruments stayed on the clay table. Signed life stayed in the mesh. The distinction held.

He walked the gallery once more in the last useful light — unfinished clay that lied about almost-finished, a cast waiting for finish, dust only when he moved — and refused again the thought that a receipt finished a life. Life continues under weather. The receipt only said what strangers were allowed to carry. He put both palms on the Code desk, felt ordinary wood and ordinary heat, and left the Antichristos stack face-down for hungrier manners tomorrow. Soft scrub tourist arguments could wait outside. Kind paint could hold without his blessing. Measurement could converge without his face in a closed room.

Before he left he checked the Honey Badger and the Rogers twin once — open, close, kinship present-tense and deep-time — and did not invent a call to the river. Concurrent consents. Concurrent desks. No shared scene required. Platform keep/unbind/ship somewhere north; custody partial rails here. Weather still blowing. Work still work. He turned off the secondary soft-ask blink without reading it, left the studio lights as they were, and stepped into Cape evening carrying knives for kinship and rails around a life — not a parish, not a crown, not a keyring sacrament. Good enough for a desk. Exact enough for consent week.

Evening came in on the water first.

# 23 — Closing note

> **[—]** · living-person care

---

This novel draws Andries J. Greyling and Gerhard van Niekerk as fiction principals from life. The outer year is 2035; the inner autobiography is life-and-work under AJ’s hand, with Lucid in the choir. Public record binds where it is cited. Private pain stays on the page only where the tellers have put it there.

Bouwer van Niekerk — G’s brother — was assassinated at his Saxonwold insolvency practice on 5 September 2025. The Law Society of South Africa condemned the killing on 8 September 2025 as a targeted attack on a legal practitioner and on the rule of law. In these pages that death is named as wound, not solved as plot. No invented assassin. No continuity-network gotcha. No thriller built on a real boardroom. Fog of commercial reporting may be noted; fog is not adopted as verdict.

Living-person care also means: no diagnosis tourism around institutionalisation; no tidy reunion invented for an unread father dedication; no sanding of stated life goals (write natural philosophy and physics; smoke dab; talk shit) into respectability brand; knives as kinship, not choreography; open attribution of Lucid to 420 Code algorithms treated as load-bearing cause of fame, not as gossip. Studio G, the mats, the ignored shelf, and the Constant landing as measurement belong to the life; they are not props for a parish or a syllabus dump.

The Four Quarters are reception weather — appetite for a face, credential weaponry, platform rage, engineering hunger for keys — not a census and not a hit on living faith communities’ doctrines as doctrine. Antichristos is satire of that appetite. The Kind’s entire public liturgy is quoted exact because the line is load-bearing:

***Don’t be a cunt — be kind.***

CongoSky.cloud embodied that ethic as operating law before the street made paint of it. This book does not ask you to join a parish. It asks you to notice custody: what stays platformed, what stays unbound, what a man will allow said about his life, and what he will not sign.

The inner autobiography is not a 420 Code textbook and not a TED arc. Physics appears where it is earned — a CODATA line, a drop-result, a derivation named inside a life. Painful stories stay under-dramatised on purpose. The flat line is the loaded one. Partial authorisation is the locked custody shape on the page: spine allowed; Bouwer pages careful and public-record bound; hospital as stigma-engine without tourism; father unread left unread; stated goals left unpolished; crowns and keyring-sacraments refused.

If you recognise yourself in these pages, the partial authorisations are the point. Careful naming. Work that stays work. Weather that is not defeated by a last chapter. Platform consent and custody consent are desk acts — keep, unbind, ship; sign some rails, refuse the crown and the keyring sacrament — not press conferences. Braid by week and theme; no forced shared room required for kinship to hold.

Readers looking for a forced AJ–G reunion, a solved assassination, a defeated schism, or a trailer for another century will not find them here. What remains is prior soil: a river, a studio, a beam named out loud, two men deciding what meaning they will carry while the mesh still hums and the alley paint still dries.

Lucid, where it appears, is near-now finished at planetary scale — cite-or-refuse, integer-quiet, no GPU fever — not an oracle and not a third skull. Hyperscaler frost and Illuminatie soft-no are pressure systems, not moustache-twirl cabals. The Kind is a sentence on walls; Antichristos is appetite for a face; both can be true on the same morning without either owning the men who refuse to become parish property.

No AFRIKA 2100 trailer lives here. This is prior soil. The river, the desk, the node still lit.
